													<script data-name="featured-video-mapped-to-wiki">
	(function () {
		// we can't use mw.config here because it's too early so we need to pass this data in different way
		const videoDetails = {
			mapping: {"mediaId":"K21eFFRc","impressionsPerSession":4},
			impressionsPerSession: 4,
		};
		const videoBridgeCountries = ["AE","AG","AL","AM","AR","AT","AU","AZ","BA","BB","BD","BG","BH","BN","BO","BR","BS","BY","CA","CH","CL","CN","CO","CR","CU","CY","CZ","DE","DK","DO","DZ","EC","EE","EG","ES","FI","FR","GB","GE","GF","GG","GH","GP","GR","GT","GU","GY","HK","HN","HR","HU","ID","IE","IL","IM","IN","IQ","IR","IS","IT","JE","JM","JO","JP","KE","KG","KH","KR","KW","KY","KZ","LA","LB","LK","LT","LU","LV","LY","MA","MD","ME","MK","MM","MN","MO","MQ","MT","MU","MV","MX","MY","NG","NI","NL","NO","NP","NZ","OM","PA","PE","PH","PK","PL","PR","PS","PT","PY","QA","RE","RO","RS","RU","SA","SC","SE","SG","SI","SK","SV","TD","TH","TN","TR","TT","TW","UA","US","UY","UZ","VE","VI","VN","ZA","ZW"];
		const tier3VideoBridgeCountries = ["GB","BE","NL","DE","AU","MX","MY","AR","FR","MD","CA","ES","IE","IT","PE","CO","CL","NZ","PL"];
		let hasVideoOnPage = null;

		function getCookieValue(cookieName) {
			const cookieSplit = ('; ' + document.cookie).split('; ' + cookieName + '=');
			return cookieSplit.length === 2 ? cookieSplit.pop().split(';').shift() : null;
		}

		function hasMaxedOutPlayerImpressionsInWiki() {
			const impressionsSoFar = Number(getCookieValue('playerImpressionsInWiki')) || 0;
			const allowedImpressions = Number(videoDetails.impressionsPerSession);

			return impressionsSoFar >= allowedImpressions;
		}

		function getCountryCode() {
			try {
				return JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(getCookieValue('Geo'))).country.toLowerCase();
			} catch {
				return null;
			}
		}

		function isVideoBridgeAllowedForCountry() {
			const countryCode = getCountryCode();

			if (!countryCode) {
				return false;
			}

			// If it's a default mapping, then check the country whitelist from the default mapping.
			if (videoDetails?.mapping?.isDefaultMapping) {
				// If the wiki is classified as a tier3 wiki, then check the country whitelist from the tier3VideoBridge whitelist.
				const allowedCountriesForTier3Wikis = tier3VideoBridgeCountries.map(function (allowedTier3CountryCode) {
					return allowedTier3CountryCode.toLowerCase();
				})

				return allowedCountriesForTier3Wikis.indexOf(countryCode) !== -1;
			} else {
				// If not a default mapping wiki (so it's a wiki mapping), then check the country whitelist from the videoBridge whitelist
				const allowedCountries = videoBridgeCountries.map(function (allowedCountryCode) {
					return allowedCountryCode.toLowerCase();
				});

				return allowedCountries.indexOf(countryCode) !== -1;
			}
		}

		window.fandomIsVideoPossible = function () {
			if (hasVideoOnPage === null) {
				hasVideoOnPage =
					videoDetails.mapping &&
					isVideoBridgeAllowedForCountry() &&
					!hasMaxedOutPlayerImpressionsInWiki();
			}

			return hasVideoOnPage;
		};

		window.canPlayVideo = function () {
			return window.fandomIsVideoPossible()
		};

		if (!window.canPlayVideo()) {
			document.body.classList.add('no-featured-video');
		} else {
			document.body.classList.add('has-featured-video');
		}
	})();
</script>

			<meta itemprop="name" content="Inside Magic: The Gathering SDCC 2026 | Collector's Quest">
		<meta itemprop="duration" content="PT04M26S">
		<meta itemprop="description" content="Step inside the Hasbro booth at San Diego Comic-Con as we sit down with Mark Rosewater, Head Designer for Magic: The Gathering, to discuss the highly anticipated Marvel collaboration!

We get an inside look at the creative brainstorming process behind bringing Earth's Mightiest Heroes to the tabletop—including how a giant whiteboard helped shape the set, the inspiration behind an unexpected &quot;deep cut&quot; character choice, and how the design team created brand-new gameplay mechanics for iconic artifacts like Thor's hammer, Mjölnir. We also explore how famous comic book locations were selected for land cards before wrapping things up by opening a fresh booster pack right on the show floor to see what cards we pull!">
		<meta itemprop="thumbnailUrl" content="https://cdn.jwplayer.com/v2/media/x49BfqzK/poster.jpg?width=720">
		<meta itemprop="contentURL" content="https://cdn.jwplayer.com/videos/x49BfqzK-cSpmBcaY.mp4">
		<meta itemprop="uploadDate" content="2026-07-29T21:17:30+00:00">
		<div id="featured-video__player-container" class="featured-video-player-container"></div>

<p style="text-align:center; color: red;"><i><b>SPOILERS AHEAD, READ AT YOUR OWN DISCRETION!</b></i></p>
<p style="text-align:center; color: #fff;">If you haven't played <a href="/wiki/Mystic_Messenger_Wikia" class="mw-redirect" title="Mystic Messenger Wikia">Mystic Messenger</a> completely, you might read something that could reveal to you major parts of the story!</p>
</div>
<p><br>
<b>Chat Rooms</b> are one of the key features of <i>Mystic Messenger</i>, where the player gets to interact with the other characters in the game. An open chat room will last until the next timing when another chat room will open. Should that happen, the player will not be able to participate in the expired chat room unless they spend 10 <a href="/wiki/Hourglasses" title="Hourglasses">Hourglasses</a> to make the chat room available again in <a href="/wiki/Casual_Story" title="Casual Story">Casual Story</a> and <a href="/wiki/Deep_Story" title="Deep Story">Deep Story</a> or 15 Hourglasses in <a href="/wiki/Another_Story" title="Another Story">Another Story</a>.
</p><p>After getting a first ending, all chats can be replayed through the "Chat History" page in the <a href="/wiki/History" title="History">History</a> section. On the right side of each chat room, there are two buttons:
</p>
<ol><li>Button containing a single speech bubble, replays the non-participating version of the chat room</li>
<li>Button containing two speech bubbles, replays the participating version of the chat room</li></ol>
<p>Either one of them will have a lock button depending on the player's participation. Both versions of the chat room can be available via two methods:
</p>
<ol><li>Purchase the chat room for 15 Hourglasses (in Another Story) or 10 Hourglasses (in all other routes) if missed during a play through</li>
<li>In a second play through, access the chat room on time if obtaining the participating version, skip the chat room if obtaining the non-participating version</li></ol>
<p>Only responses that the player selected in a play through of the game will be available.
</p><p><span class="new" title="Template:See also (page does not exist)" data-uncrawlable-url="L3dpa2kvVGVtcGxhdGU6U2VlX2Fsc28/YWN0aW9uPWVkaXQmcmVkbGluaz0x">Template:See also</span>
</p>

<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="Prologue">Prologue</span></h2>
<p>The prologue can be played at any time of the day, due to the fact that the 1st Day has the most chats out of the eleven days. However, it is important to note that the time factor still exists. Depending on what time the player starts the game, they might not be able to get 100% on the first day.
</p><p><i>Note:&nbsp;SM&nbsp;= Story Mode, formerly known as Visual Novel</i>
</p>

<h2><span class="mw-headline" id="After_Ending">After Ending</span></h2>
<p>Once the player has obtained a character's Good Ending, they will be able to unlock their After Ending for 50 Hourglasses. All progress saved will be saved in the Extra -&gt; Chat History pathway from the Main Screen.
</p>

<h2><span id="Valentine.27s_Day_DLC"></span><span class="mw-headline" id="Valentine's_Day_DLC"><a href="/wiki/Valentine%27s_Day_DLC" title="Valentine's Day DLC">Valentine's Day DLC</a></span></h2>
<p>Once the player has obtained a characters good ending they may unlock the Valentines Day After Endings for 20 HG each, these can be played anytime and it won't be accepted; even if the After Endings aren't bought.
</p>
